Mathlib.Data.Ordmap.Invariants

Ordnode.BalanceddefMathlib.Data.Ordmap.Invariants, predicate
Wording
x1 is balanced (from the name)
English
Let $x_{1}$ be an element of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$. Then $x_{1}$ is balanced, if $\operatorname{brecOn}$ holds for $x_{1}$ and $\mathrm{Ordnode\_\_Balanced\_\_\_f}$.
Formula
$x_{1} \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x)\vdash \operatorname{Balanced} (x_{1}):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{brecOn} (x_{1} , \mathrm{Ordnode\_\_Balanced\_\_\_f})$
French
Soit $x_{1}$ un élément de $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ appliqué à $x$. Alors $x_{1}$ est équilibré si $\operatorname{brecOn}$ tient pour $x_{1}$ et $\mathrm{Ordnode\_\_Balanced\_\_\_f}$.
Ordnode.BalancedSzdefMathlib.Data.Ordmap.Invariants, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $l$ and $r$ be natural numbers. Then $\operatorname{BalancedSz}$ holds for $l$ and $r$, if $l + r \leq 1$ or $l$ is less than or equal to the product of the delta and $r$ and $r$ is less than or equal to the product of the delta and $l$.
Formula
$l , r \in \Natural \vdash \operatorname{BalancedSz} (l , r):\Leftrightarrow l + r \leq 1 \vee l \leq \operatorname{delta} \times r \wedge r \leq \operatorname{delta} \times l$
French
Soient $l$ et $r$ des nombres naturels. Alors $\operatorname{BalancedSz}$ tient pour $l$ et $r$ si $l + r \leq 1$ ou $l$ est inférieur ou égal au produit du delta et de $r$ et $r$ est inférieur ou égal au produit du delta et de $l$.
Ordnode.BoundeddefMathlib.Data.Ordmap.Invariants, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{2}$ be an element of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$. Let $x_{3}$ be an element of $\mathrm{WithBot}$ applied to $x$. Let $x_{4}$ be an element of $\mathrm{WithTop}$ applied to $x$. Then $\operatorname{Bounded}$ holds for $x_{2}$, $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$, if $\operatorname{brecOn}$ holds for $x_{2}$, $\mathrm{Ordnode\_\_Bounded\_\_\_f}$, $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$.
Formula
$x_{2} \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x), x_{3} \in \mathrm{WithBot} (x), x_{4} \in \mathrm{WithTop} (x)\vdash \operatorname{Bounded} (x_{2} , x_{3} , x_{4}):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{brecOn} (x_{2} , \mathrm{Ordnode\_\_Bounded\_\_\_f} , x_{3} , x_{4})$
French
Soit $x_{2}$ un élément de $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ appliqué à $x$. Soit $x_{3}$ un élément de $\mathrm{WithBot}$ appliqué à $x$. Soit $x_{4}$ un élément de $\mathrm{WithTop}$ appliqué à $x$. Alors $\operatorname{Bounded}$ tient pour $x_{2}$, $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$ si $\operatorname{brecOn}$ tient pour $x_{2}$, $\mathrm{Ordnode\_\_Bounded\_\_\_f}$, $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$.
Ordnode.RaiseddefMathlib.Data.Ordmap.Invariants, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $n$ and $m$ be natural numbers. Then $\operatorname{Raised}$ holds for $n$ and $m$, if $m = n$ or $m = n + 1$.
Formula
$n , m \in \Natural \vdash \operatorname{Raised} (n , m):\Leftrightarrow m = n \vee m = n + 1$
French
Soient $n$ et $m$ des nombres naturels. Alors $\operatorname{Raised}$ tient pour $n$ et $m$ si $m = n$ ou $m = n + 1$.

Wording
the balance of x1 and r (from the name)
English
Let $l$ and $r$ be elements of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$. Let $x_{1}$ be an element of $x$. Then the balance of $x_{1}$ and $r$ is an element of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$ defined as $\iteMACRoG {\mathrm{Leq} (\operatorname{size} (l)+ \operatorname{size} (r), 1)}{\operatorname{node'} (x_{1} , r)}{(\iteMACRoG {\mathrm{Gt} (\operatorname{size} (r), \operatorname{delta} \times \operatorname{size} (l))}{\operatorname{rotateL} (x_{1} , r)}{(\iteMACRoG {\mathrm{Gt} (\operatorname{size} (l), \operatorname{delta} \times \operatorname{size} (r))}{\operatorname{rotateR} (x_{1} , r)}{\operatorname{node'} (x_{1} , r)})})}$.
Formula
$l \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x), x_{1} \in x , r \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x)\vdash \operatorname{balance'} (l , x_{1} , r):= \iteMACRoI {\mathrm{Leq} (\operatorname{size} (l)+ \operatorname{size} (r), 1)}{\operatorname{node'} (l , x_{1} , r)}{(\iteMACRoI {\mathrm{Gt} (\operatorname{size} (r), \operatorname{delta} \times \operatorname{size} (l))}{\operatorname{rotateL} (l , x_{1} , r)}{(\iteMACRoI {\mathrm{Gt} (\operatorname{size} (l), \operatorname{delta} \times \operatorname{size} (r))}{\operatorname{rotateR} (l , x_{1} , r)}{\operatorname{node'} (l , x_{1} , r)})})}\in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x)$
French
Soient $l$ et $r$ des éléments de $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ appliqué à $x$. Soit $x_{1}$ un élément de $x$. Alors l'équilibre de $x_{1}$ et $r$ est un élément de $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ appliqué à $x$ défini comme $\iteMACRoG {\mathrm{Leq} (\operatorname{size} (l)+ \operatorname{size} (r), 1)}{\operatorname{node'} (x_{1} , r)}{(\iteMACRoG {\mathrm{Gt} (\operatorname{size} (r), \operatorname{delta} \times \operatorname{size} (l))}{\operatorname{rotateL} (x_{1} , r)}{(\iteMACRoG {\mathrm{Gt} (\operatorname{size} (l), \operatorname{delta} \times \operatorname{size} (r))}{\operatorname{rotateR} (x_{1} , r)}{\operatorname{node'} (x_{1} , r)})})}$.

Ordnode.node3LdefMathlib.Data.Ordmap.Invariants, function or constant
Wording
the node of y and r (from the name)
English
Let $l$, $m$ and $r$ be elements of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$. Let $x_{1}$ and $y$ be elements of $x$. Then the node of $y$ and $r$ is an element of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$ defined as the node of $y$ and $r$.
Formula
$l \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x), x_{1} \in x , m \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x), y \in x , r \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x)\vdash \operatorname{node3L} (l , x_{1} , m , y , r):= \operatorname{node'} (\operatorname{node'} (l , x_{1} , m), y , r)\in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x)$

Ordnode.AmemdefMathlib.Data.Ordmap.Ordnode, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{2}$ be an element of $x$. Let $x_{3}$ be an element of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$. Then $\operatorname{Amem}$ holds for $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$, if $\operatorname{Any}$ holds for the function that maps $y$ to the proposition that $x_{2} \leq y \leq x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$.
Formula
$x_{2} \in x , x_{3} \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x)\vdash \operatorname{Amem} (x_{2} , x_{3}):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{Any} (\lambda y . x_{2} \leq y \wedge y \leq x_{2} , x_{3})$
French
Soit $x_{2}$ un élément de $x$. Soit $x_{3}$ un élément de $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ appliqué à $x$. Alors $\operatorname{Amem}$ tient pour $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$ si $\operatorname{Any}$ tient pour la fonction qui envoie $y$ à la proposition que $x_{2} \leq y \leq x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$.

Ordnode.EquivdefMathlib.Data.Ordmap.Ordnode, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
Let $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ be elements of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$. Then $\operatorname{Equiv}$ holds for $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$, if $\operatorname{size} (x_{1})= \operatorname{size} (x_{2})$ and the list of $x_{1}$ is equal to the list of $x_{2}$.
Formula
$x_{1} , x_{2} \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x)\vdash \operatorname{Equiv} (x_{1} , x_{2}):\Leftrightarrow \operatorname{size} (x_{1})= \operatorname{size} (x_{2})\wedge (\EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{toList} (x_{1})}{\operatorname{toList} (x_{2})})$
French
Soient $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ des éléments de $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ appliqué à $x$. Alors $\operatorname{Equiv}$ tient pour $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ si $\operatorname{size} (x_{1})= \operatorname{size} (x_{2})$ et la liste de $x_{1}$ est égale à la liste de $x_{2}$.
Ordnode.adjustWithdefMathlib.Data.Ordmap.Ordnode, function or constant
Wording
no wording
English
Let $f$ be a function from elements of $x$ to elements of $x$. Let $x_{3}$ be an element of $x$. Let $x_{4}$ be an element of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$. Then $\operatorname{adjustWith}$ applied to $f$, $x_{3}$ and $x_{4}$ is an element of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$ defined as $\operatorname{brecOn}$ applied to $x_{4}$ and $\mathrm{Ordnode\_\_adjustWith\_\_\_f}$ applied to $f$ and $x_{3}$.
Formula
$f \in x \rightarrow x , x_{3} \in x , x_{4} \in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x)\vdash \operatorname{adjustWith} (f , x_{3} , x_{4}):= \operatorname{brecOn} (x_{4} , \mathrm{Ordnode\_\_adjustWith\_\_\_f} (f , x_{3}))\in \mathrm{Ordnode} (x)$
French
Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $x$ à des éléments de $x$. Soit $x_{3}$ un élément de $x$. Soit $x_{4}$ un élément de $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ appliqué à $x$. Alors $\operatorname{adjustWith}$ appliqué à $f$, $x_{3}$ et $x_{4}$ est un élément de $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ appliqué à $x$ défini comme $\operatorname{brecOn}$ appliqué à $x_{4}$ et $\mathrm{Ordnode\_\_adjustWith\_\_\_f}$ appliqué à $f$ et $x_{3}$.

Wording
the alteration of x3 and x4 (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English

Let $x_{4}$ be an element of $\mathrm{Ordnode}$ applied to $x$. Let $n$ be a natural number. Let $A$ be a set. Then the constant of $n$ and $A$ is a multivariate functor of $n$ defined as $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $A$ and the function that maps $x$ and $x_{1}$ to $\mathrm{PEmpty}$.
Formula
$n \in \Natural , A \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{const} (n , A):= \operatorname{mk} (A , \lambda x , x_{1} . \mathrm{PEmpty})\in \mathrm{MvPFunctor} (n)$
French
Soit $n$ un nombre naturel. Soit $A$ un ensemble. Alors la constante de $n$ et $A$ est un foncteur multivarié de $n$ défini comme $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $A$ et la fonction qui envoie $x$ et $x_{1}$ à $\mathrm{PEmpty}$.

English
Let $x_{1}$ be an object of the constant of $n$ and $A$ and $x$. Then $\operatorname{get}$ applied to $x_{1}$ is an element of $A$ defined as $\operatorname{fst}$ applied to $x_{1}$.

MvPFunctor.mapdefMathlib.Data.PFunctor.Multivariate.Basic, function or constant
Wording
the map of f and x2 (from the name)
English
Let $P$ be a multivariate functor of $n$. Let $f$ be an arrow of $x$ and $x_{1}$. Let $x_{2}$ be an object of $P$ and $x$. Then the map of $f$ and $x_{2}$ is an object of $P$ and $x_{1}$.
Formula
$P \in \mathrm{MvPFunctor} (n), f \in \operatorname{Arrow} (x , x_{1}), x_{2} \in \operatorname{Obj} (P , x)\vdash \operatorname{map} (P , f , x_{2})\in \operatorname{Obj} (P , x_{1})$
French

MvPFunctor.MdefMathlib.Data.PFunctor.Multivariate.M, type
Wording
no wording
English
Let $P$ be a multivariate functor of $n + 1$. Let $x$ be a type vector of $n$. Then an element of $\operatorname{M}$ applied to $P$ and $x$ is an element of the function that maps $P$ and $x$ to the set of objects of $\operatorname{mp}$ applied to $P$ and $x$.
Formula
$P \in \mathrm{MvPFunctor} (n + 1), x \in \mathrm{TypeVec} (n)\vdash \operatorname{M} (P , x):= \lambda P , x . \operatorname{Obj} (\operatorname{mp} (P), x)$
French
Soit $P$ un foncteur multivarié de $n + 1$. Soit $x$ un vecteur de types de $n$. Alors un élément de $\operatorname{M}$ appliqué à $P$ et $x$ est un élément de la fonction qui envoie $P$ et $x$ à l'ensemble d'objets de $\operatorname{mp}$ appliqué à $P$ et $x$.

Formula
$P \in \mathrm{MvPFunctor} (n + 1), x \in \operatorname{M} (\operatorname{last} (P)), x_{1} \in \mathrm{Fin2} (n)\vdash \operatorname{Path} (P , x , x_{1})\ \mathsf{set}$

MvPFunctor.M.Path.childconstructorMathlib.Data.PFunctor.Multivariate.M, function or constant
Wording
the child of i and c (from the name)
English
Let $x$ be an element of $\operatorname{M}$ applied to $\operatorname{last}$ applied to $P$. Let $a$ be an element of $\operatorname{A}$ applied to $P$. Let $f$ be a function from elements of $\operatorname{B}$ applied to $\operatorname{last}$ applied to $P$ and $a$ to elements of $\operatorname{M}$ applied to $\operatorname{last}$ applied to $P$ such that the destructor of $x$ is equal to $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $a$ and $f$. Let $c$ be a path of $f$ applied to $j$ and $i$. Then the child of $i$ and $c$ is a path of $x$ and $i$.
Formula
$x \in \operatorname{M} (\operatorname{last} (P)), a \in \operatorname{A} (P), f \in \operatorname{B} (\operatorname{last} (P), a)\rightarrow \operatorname{M} (\operatorname{last} (P)), \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{dest} (x)}{\operatorname{mk} (a , f)}, j \in \operatorname{B} (\operatorname{last} (P), a), i \in \mathrm{Fin2} (n), c \in \operatorname{Path} (P , f (j), i)\vdash \operatorname{child} (x , a , f , h , j , i , c)\in \operatorname{Path} (P , x , i)$
French
Soit $x$ un élément de $\operatorname{M}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{last}$ appliqué à $P$. Soit $a$ un élément de $\operatorname{A}$ appliqué à $P$. Soit $f$ une fonction des éléments de $\operatorname{B}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{last}$ appliqué à $P$ et $a$ à des éléments de $\operatorname{M}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{last}$ appliqué à $P$ telle que le destructeur de $x$ soit égal à $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $a$ et $f$. Soit $j$ un élément de $\operatorname{B}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{last}$ appliqué à $P$ et $a$. Soit $i$ un élément de $\mathrm{Fin2}$ appliqué à $n$. Soit $c$ un chemin de $f$ appliqué à $j$ et $i$. Alors l'enfant de $i$ et $c$ est un chemin de $x$ et $i$.

English

MvPFunctor.M.corecdefMathlib.Data.PFunctor.Multivariate.M, function or constant
Wording
the corecursor of g and x2 (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $P$ be a multivariate functor of $n + 1$. Let $g$ be a function from elements of $x_{1}$ to objects of $P$ and $\operatorname{append1}$ applied to $x$ and $x_{1}$. Let $x_{2}$ be an element of $x_{1}$. Then the corecursor of $g$ and $x_{2}$ is an element of $\operatorname{M}$ applied to $P$ and $x$ defined as the corecursor of the function that maps $b_{2}$ to $\operatorname{lastFun}$ applied to $\operatorname{snd}$ applied to $g$ applied to $b_{2}$ and $x_{2}$.
Formula
$P \in \mathrm{MvPFunctor} (n + 1), g \in x_{1} \rightarrow \operatorname{Obj} (P , \operatorname{append1} (x , x_{1})), x_{2} \in x_{1} \vdash \operatorname{corec} (P , g , x_{2}):= \operatorname{corec'} (P , \lambda b . \operatorname{fst} (g (b)), \lambda b_{1} . \operatorname{dropFun} (\operatorname{snd} (g (b_{1}))), \lambda b_{2} . \operatorname{lastFun} (\operatorname{snd} (g (b_{2}))), x_{2})\in \operatorname{M} (P , x)$
French
Soit $P$ un foncteur multivarié de $n + 1$. Soit $g$ une fonction des éléments de $x_{1}$ à des objets de $P$ et $\operatorname{append1}$ appliqué à $x$ et $x_{1}$. Alors le corécurseur de $g$ et $x_{2}$ est un élément de $\operatorname{M}$ appliqué à $P$ et $x$ défini comme le corécurseur de la fonction qui envoie $b_{2}$ à $\operatorname{lastFun}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{snd}$ appliqué à $g$ appliqué à $b_{2}$ et $x_{2}$.

the corecursor content of x4 and b1 (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $P$ be a multivariate functor of $n + 1$. Let $x_{2}$ be a function from elements of $x_{1}$ to elements of $\operatorname{A}$ applied to $P$. MvPFunctor.M.corecShapedefMathlib.Data.PFunctor.Multivariate.M, function or constant
Wording
the corecursor shape of x2 and b (from the name, with words of the lexicon)
English
Let $P$ be a multivariate functor of $n + 1$. Let $x_{1}$ be a function from elements of $x$ to elements of $\operatorname{A}$ applied to $P$. Let $x_{2}$ be a function from elements $b$ of $x$ and elements of $\operatorname{B}$ applied to $\operatorname{last}$ applied to $P$ and $x_{1}$ applied to $b$ to elements of $x$. PFunctor.AdefMathlib.Data.PFunctor.Univariate.Basic, type
Wording
no wording
English

MvQPF.IsUniformdefMathlib.Data.QPF.Multivariate.Basic, predicate
Wording
no wording
English
$\operatorname{IsUniform}$, if for all type vectors $x$ of $n$, for all elements $a$ and $\mathrm{a'}$ of $\operatorname{A}$ applied to $\operatorname{P}$ applied to $F$, for all arrows $f$ of $\operatorname{B}$ applied to $\operatorname{P}$ applied to $F$ and $a$ and $x$, for all arrows $\mathrm{f'}$ of $\operatorname{B}$ applied to $\operatorname{P}$ applied to $F$ and $\mathrm{a'}$ and $x$, if the absolute value of $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $a$ and $f$ is equal to the absolute value of $\operatorname{mk}$ applied to $\mathrm{a'}$ and $\mathrm{f'}$, then for all elements $i$ of $\mathrm{Fin2}$ applied to $n$, the image of $f$ applied to $i$ and the universal set is equal to the image of $\mathrm{f'}$ applied to $i$ and the universal set.
Formula
$\operatorname{IsUniform} :\Leftrightarrow \forall x \in \mathrm{TypeVec} (n). \forall a \in \operatorname{A} (\operatorname{P} (F)). \forall \mathrm{a'} \in \operatorname{A} (\operatorname{P} (F)). \forall f \in \operatorname{Arrow} (\operatorname{B} (\operatorname{P} (F), a), x). \forall \mathrm{f'} \in \operatorname{Arrow} (\operatorname{B} (\operatorname{P} (F), \mathrm{a'}), x). \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{abs} (\operatorname{mk} (a , f))}{\operatorname{abs} (\operatorname{mk} (\mathrm{a'} , \mathrm{f'}))}\rightarrow (\forall i \in \mathrm{Fin2} (n). \EqtMACRoJ {\operatorname{image} (f (i), \operatorname{univ})}{\operatorname{image} (\mathrm{f'} (i), \operatorname{univ})})$
French
$\operatorname{IsUniform}$ si pour tous les vecteurs de types $x$ de $n$, pour tous les éléments $a$ et $\mathrm{a'}$ de $\operatorname{A}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{P}$ appliqué à $F$, pour toutes les flèches $f$ de $\operatorname{B}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{P}$ appliqué à $F$ et $a$ et $x$, pour toutes les flèches $\mathrm{f'}$ de $\operatorname{B}$ appliqué à $\operatorname{P}$ appliqué à $F$ et $\mathrm{a'}$ et $x$, si la valeur absolue de $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $a$ et $f$ est égale à la valeur absolue de $\operatorname{mk}$ appliqué à $\mathrm{a'}$ et $\mathrm{f'}$, alors pour tous les éléments $i$ de $\mathrm{Fin2}$ appliqué à $n$, l'image de $f$ appliqué à $i$ et l'ensemble universel est égale à l'image de $\mathrm{f'}$ appliqué à $i$ et l'ensemble universel.
